### Coverage Limitations

官方 Atom `published` 字段证明 first submission time；snapshot 的 observed version 可能晚于 v1，因此只承担 identity/recall，不承担候选机制 claim。28 项 retained claim 均已绑定 exact-v1 HTML/PDF；`2606.09864v1` 当前 metadata 已出现后续版本，因此机制与实验只引用官方 v1 PDF。`2606.28343v1` 也以官方 v1 PDF 复核。arXiv 仍是作者稿，不等于 peer review 或生产复现；未披露的 immutable artifact commit、线上并发与生产 SLO 不由作者 benchmark 反推。Required Daily 其他来源的 Effective Date 晚于本历史窗口，不追溯为到期源。

### Source Reviews

<!-- review:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:start -->
### Characterizing Metastable Faults and Failures

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Local stabilizers are reasonable when faults compose independently, but circular reactions can keep a distributed system oscillating even though every component is trying to recover. Symptom-by-symptom mitigation then needs a global liveness boundary.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Metastable faults arise from circular destabilizing interactions among individually stabilizing components; tentative scheduling of destabilizing actions and deferral until global stabilization changes the recovery contract from symptom mitigation to composition-aware liveness control. Each component retains authority over its local state transition. A composition-aware scheduler owns tentative admission of potentially destabilizing actions, the dependency/cycle model and the global-stabilization predicate; only after that predicate holds may it commit or release deferred work. Nyx is a model/checking aid, not production-state authority. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00942v1 public exact-v1 HTML https://arxiv.org/html/2606.00942v1 § `3 Model Sketch`; § `4 Metastable Faults`; § `5 Metastable Failures`; § `6 Achieving Metastable Fault Tolerance``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.00942v1 does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00942v1 public exact-v1 HTML https://arxiv.org/html/2606.00942v1 § `7 Case Study: Oscillating Membership`; Appendix `B The Look-Aside Cache Incident``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00942v1 public exact-v1 HTML https://arxiv.org/html/2606.00942v1 § `9 The Road Ahead``。The formal model establishes metastable faults/failures relative to its adversary and scheduling assumptions, and the three case studies demonstrate the analysis pattern. They do not prove completeness for arbitrary feedback loops or fidelity between every Nyx abstraction and implementation. 原文边界保持为：The formal result is relative to the paper's system model and adversaries, and the three case studies do not prove that the methodology detects every production feedback loop or that Nyx models preserve every implementation detail.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Tentative scheduling can preserve liveness but increases latency, model maintenance and the risk of deferring useful work. An incomplete dependency graph can miss a cycle; an over-conservative predicate can deadlock recovery. Independent faults and well-bounded local retries remain valid on the simpler path.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Recovery evolves from reactive component health to composition-level stabilization evidence; the next pressure is deriving and validating the dependency model from live traces without making the model itself a single point of failure. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:start -->Metastable faults arise from circular destabilizing interactions among individually stabilizing components; tentative scheduling of destabilizing actions and deferral until global stabilization changes the recovery contract from symptom mitigation to composition-aware liveness control. 证据边界：The formal result is relative to the paper's system model and adversaries, and the three case studies do not prove that the methodology detects every production feedback loop or that Nyx models preserve every implementation detail.<!-- claim:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:end -->
<!-- review:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:end -->

<!-- review:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:start -->
### PRISM: Gauge-Invariant Tangent-Space Differentially Private LoRA

<!-- claim:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:start -->直接在 LoRA 的 A/B 因子上做 DP-SGD，在固定参数化下看似自然，但同一低秩更新存在多种 gauge 等价分解，噪声会被双线性放大。PRISM 改在低秩矩阵的内禀切空间采样 gauge-invariant 噪声，并让自适应更新不再放大该噪声。 形式化 DP 保证与数值实验支持“扰动应绑定可辨识更新而非任意因子”的边界；它不证明所有 rank、optimizer 和 privacy budget 下都优于普通 LoRA。内禀采样与几何计算增加实现复杂度，无隐私要求或 factor gauge 已固定时旧方案仍更简单。<!-- claim:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:end -->
Deep evidence contract：Method/identity=`arXiv:2606.00944v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3 Proposed Method: PRISM`; § `3.1`–`3.3``；Evaluation=`arXiv:2606.00944v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experiments`; Appendix `B Experimental Setup`; Appendix `C Additional Diagnostics and Analysis``；Limitations/counterevidence=`arXiv:2606.00944v1 exact-v1 HTML § `Limitations`; Appendix `C Additional Diagnostics and Analysis``；Artifact=`Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.00944v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。Owner/authority：`PRISM: Gauge-Invariant Tangent-Space Differentially Private LoRA` 的长期机制归入 `TRAIN-LORA`；adapter factorization、rank、privacy accountant 与 base identity 是一个 release contract；DP guarantee 由 accountant 和具体 update parameterization 裁定。
<!-- review:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:end -->

<!-- review:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:start -->
### Lodestar: An Online-Learning LLM Inference Router

<!-- claim:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:start -->round-robin、queue-aware 和 prefix-cache-aware routing 在请求分布与 GPU 同质时合理；agentic prompt、batch/KV 耦合和异构加速器使静态启发式失准。Lodestar 按请求采集 instance/request/performance snapshot，在线训练 reward predictor，再以预测 TTFT 选择实例。 公有云 GPU 实验与 prefix/load heuristic 的比较支持特定 workload 下的 TTFT 改善和约五分钟适应速度；它不证明 predictor 在突变、观测延迟或 reward drift 下仍安全。在线训练、探索和隔离带来 control-plane 成本，冷启动与失准时应回退确定性 router。<!-- claim:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:end -->
Deep evidence contract：Method/identity=`arXiv:2606.00946v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Lodestar Design``；Evaluation=`arXiv:2606.00946v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Evaluation``；Limitations/counterevidence=`arXiv:2606.00946v1 exact-v1 HTML § `7 Conclusion` — no dedicated limitations heading; the cited closing section was read and the Daily claim boundary records the source-specific non-result`；Artifact=`Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.00946v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。Owner/authority：`Lodestar: An Online-Learning LLM Inference Router` 的长期机制归入 `INFER-SCHEDULING`；online predictor 可消费 queue/KV/workload snapshot，但实际 queue、cache locality、admission 与 fallback 状态仍由 serving runtime 拥有。
<!-- review:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:end -->

<!-- review:SF-FED-PERSONALIZATION-SILENT-FAILURES:start -->
### Silent Failures in Federated Personalization of Foundation Models

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Aggregate validation is sufficient when the server can inspect representative behavior, but federated personalization hides client-local outcomes behind privacy and aggregation. Bias, miscalibration, fairness loss or alignment erosion can therefore pass a healthy global average.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Federated foundation-model personalization makes client-level behavior inaccessible to a central observer, so bias, miscalibration, fairness collapse, adaptation misalignment, out-of-domain degradation, and alignment erosion can pass ordinary aggregate acceptance; the durable delta is a privacy-preserving behavioral-evaluation contract that assigns what may be observed, aggregated, audited, and used for release. Clients own raw examples and local behavioral outcomes; the measurement protocol owns which slice statistics may leave a client, their privacy budget and aggregation version; the evaluator owns audit tests; the release controller owns acceptance. No central scorer may infer unavailable client facts or treat an aggregate as a complete detector. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00947v1 exact-v1 HTML § `2. Problem Formulation``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.00947v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.00947v1 exact-v1 HTML has no separately identifiable empirical evaluation section`；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00947v1 exact-v1 HTML § `6. Conclusion``。The exact-v1 taxonomy identifies distinct silent-failure classes and derives a privacy-preserving evaluation agenda. It contains no separately identifiable empirical monitor evaluation, so it proves neither detector completeness nor production thresholds nor that useful client-local detection is always compatible with the promised privacy level. 原文边界保持为：This exact-v1 paper is a taxonomy and research vision rather than an implemented monitor or newly executed benchmark; it does not establish complete detectors, production thresholds, or that privacy-preserving statistics can identify every client-local failure without weakening privacy.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Finer slices improve visibility but spend privacy budget, increase communication and may re-identify small cohorts; coarser aggregation can hide concentrated harm. False reassurance is the central failure mode. Ordinary aggregate acceptance remains a bounded baseline only when personalization variance and client-local risk are demonstrably low.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Evaluation evolves from one fleet score to a contract over observable client slices, privacy and release authority; the next pressure is implementing detectors with measurable false-negative and privacy-loss bounds. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `Integrate`。

<!-- claim:SF-FED-PERSONALIZATION-SILENT-FAILURES:start -->Federated foundation-model personalization makes client-level behavior inaccessible to a central observer, so bias, miscalibration, fairness collapse, adaptation misalignment, out-of-domain degradation, and alignment erosion can pass ordinary aggregate acceptance; the durable delta is a privacy-preserving behavioral-evaluation contract that assigns what may be observed, aggregated, audited, and used for release. 证据边界：This exact-v1 paper is a taxonomy and research vision rather than an implemented monitor or newly executed benchmark; it does not establish complete detectors, production thresholds, or that privacy-preserving statistics can identify every client-local failure without weakening privacy.<!-- claim:SF-FED-PERSONALIZATION-SILENT-FAILURES:end -->
<!-- review:SF-FED-PERSONALIZATION-SILENT-FAILURES:end -->

<!-- review:SF-COHESION-AWARE-MAS-PARTITIONING:start -->
### When Parallelism Pays Off: Cohesion-Aware Task Partitioning for Multi-Agent Coding

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Assigning files evenly across coding agents appears parallel but ignores symbol dependencies, structural hubs and merge order. More agents then create duplicated work and conflicts rather than shorter critical paths.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Co-Coder materializes repository work as a weighted dependency graph, isolates structural hubs, partitions cohesive file/symbol communities, and executes only dependency-ready units in parallel, making critical-path and merge-interface ownership explicit instead of equating more coding agents with more useful parallelism. The partitioner owns a versioned weighted file/symbol dependency graph and cohesive communities; the scheduler owns dependency-ready admission; agents own only their assigned worktree changes; build/tests and merge control own integration truth. A worker cannot mark a blocked unit ready from its own completion message. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00953v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3 The Co-Coder Framework``；artifact 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00953v1 exact-v1 HTML § `Appendix B Implementation Details`; no immutable commit inferred beyond the manuscript`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00953v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Results``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00953v1 exact-v1 HTML § `6 Discussion``。DevEval and CodeProjectEval results support the tested graph heuristics and agents on repositories with separable boundaries. They do not show that a graph remains accurate through dynamic edits or that parallelism helps tightly coupled or differently structured codebases. 原文边界保持为：The evidence is limited to DevEval and CodeProjectEval repositories, graph-construction heuristics, and the tested agents; it does not prove that cohesion estimates remain correct under dynamic edits or that parallelism pays outside tasks with separable dependency boundaries.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Dependency analysis and hub isolation reduce merge contention but add graph-construction cost and stale-edge risk. A missed edge permits unsafe concurrency; an overly dense graph serializes useful work. Single-agent or coarse module ownership remains better for small or highly coupled changes.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Multi-agent coding evolves from worker-count scaling to critical-path admission over repository structure; the next pressure is incremental graph repair as agents modify the very dependencies used for scheduling. 关系限定为 `Principle Reuse`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-COHESION-AWARE-MAS-PARTITIONING:start -->Co-Coder materializes repository work as a weighted dependency graph, isolates structural hubs, partitions cohesive file/symbol communities, and executes only dependency-ready units in parallel, making critical-path and merge-interface ownership explicit instead of equating more coding agents with more useful parallelism. 证据边界：The evidence is limited to DevEval and CodeProjectEval repositories, graph-construction heuristics, and the tested agents; it does not prove that cohesion estimates remain correct under dynamic edits or that parallelism pays outside tasks with separable dependency boundaries.<!-- claim:SF-COHESION-AWARE-MAS-PARTITIONING:end -->
<!-- review:SF-COHESION-AWARE-MAS-PARTITIONING:end -->

<!-- review:SF-ASYNC-AUTOFORMAL-PLANNING:start -->
### Robust Asynchronous Planning via Auto-Formalization

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Direct natural-language plans are flexible, and sequential execution hides many races. Once actions overlap, implicit resource, precedence and state constraints can be violated even when each sentence looks plausible.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Auto-formalization converts asynchronous natural-language planning problems into CP-SAT or PDDL obligations whose resource, precedence, state, and online-repair constraints are checked before concurrent actions are committed, separating proposal generation from machine-owned feasibility state. The formalizer writes a candidate CP-SAT/PDDL model with resource, precedence and state predicates; the solver owns satisfiability for that encoding; the executor owns real-world action commit and online observations. Repair produces a new versioned obligation set rather than mutating an already-committed plan invisibly. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00981v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3 Task Formulation``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.00981v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00981v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experimental Setup``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00981v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Results and Discussion``。The disclosed offline and online task families show when formalization plus a solver improves feasibility and where online extraction remains brittle. Solver validity proves only the encoded obligations, not faithful extraction, adequate expressiveness or omitted physical effects. 原文边界保持为：Correctness is bounded by extraction faithfulness, solver expressiveness, and the disclosed action/state/online task families; online formalization remains brittle, and a solver-valid encoding can still be semantically wrong or omit real-world effects.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Machine-checkable feasibility adds solver latency, schema work and a second semantic failure surface. A mistranslated constraint can make a wrong plan look certified; an over-constrained model can reject feasible concurrency. Direct or sequential planning remains reasonable for short, reversible tasks.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Planning evolves from a generated action list to proposal → formal obligation → checked admission → observed repair; the next pressure is grounding solver state in authoritative tool/environment observations. 关系限定为 `Layering / Dependency`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-ASYNC-AUTOFORMAL-PLANNING:start -->Auto-formalization converts asynchronous natural-language planning problems into CP-SAT or PDDL obligations whose resource, precedence, state, and online-repair constraints are checked before concurrent actions are committed, separating proposal generation from machine-owned feasibility state. 证据边界：Correctness is bounded by extraction faithfulness, solver expressiveness, and the disclosed action/state/online task families; online formalization remains brittle, and a solver-valid encoding can still be semantically wrong or omit real-world effects.<!-- claim:SF-ASYNC-AUTOFORMAL-PLANNING:end -->
<!-- review:SF-ASYNC-AUTOFORMAL-PLANNING:end -->

<!-- review:SF-ORDER-AGNOSTIC-CHAIN-RULE:start -->
### Decoding in Order-Agnostic Language Models: Chain-Rule Deviation and Uniform Spreading

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Autoregressive decoding uses one causal factorization, so every next-token conditional belongs to the same joint distribution. An order-agnostic masked model instead exposes conditionals under many revealed subsets; treating reveal order as a cost-only scheduler choice can therefore change the scored sequence itself.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Order-agnostic masked language models expose conditionals that need not compose into one coherent joint distribution; reveal order therefore becomes part of the decoding contract rather than a harmless scheduler choice. The durable state is the revealed-position set together with its order, the conditioning mask, the model revision and the still-mutable tokens. The model owns conditional predictions; the decoder owns reveal scheduling and commit; a likelihood evaluator may compare orders but cannot retroactively declare incompatible conditionals coherent. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00997v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4.1 Setup and Strategies``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.00997v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00997v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.00997v1 exact-v1 HTML § `6 Limitations``。The reported likelihood shifts and uniform-spreading measurements establish order sensitivity for the evaluated models and schedules. They do not establish that every discrete-diffusion model lacks a coherent joint, nor that autoregression dominates on quality or serving cost. 原文边界保持为：Reported likelihood shifts and uniform-spreading effects are specific to evaluated models and schedules; they do not prove that every diffusion language model is incoherent or inferior to autoregression.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Recording and checking reveal history makes scoring and replay auditable, but consumes metadata and may reduce parallel freedom. A scheduler that ignores the identity can silently compare different distributions; a checker that is too strict can erase the latency benefit. Causal AR and block-causal decoding remain the simpler branches when one fixed factorization is required.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** The mechanism moves the chapter from “masked positions can be updated in parallel” to “mutable-token scheduling is part of probabilistic identity”; the next pressure is a commit/checker interface shared by training likelihood, offline evaluation and serving. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `Integrate`。

<!-- claim:SF-ORDER-AGNOSTIC-CHAIN-RULE:start -->Order-agnostic masked language models expose conditionals that need not compose into one coherent joint distribution; reveal order therefore becomes part of the decoding contract rather than a harmless scheduler choice. 证据边界：Reported likelihood shifts and uniform-spreading effects are specific to evaluated models and schedules; they do not prove that every diffusion language model is incoherent or inferior to autoregression.<!-- claim:SF-ORDER-AGNOSTIC-CHAIN-RULE:end -->
<!-- review:SF-ORDER-AGNOSTIC-CHAIN-RULE:end -->

<!-- review:SF-TASK-AWARE-MOE-GROUPING:start -->
### Beyond Task-Agnostic: Task-Aware Grouping for Communication-Efficient Multi-Task MoE Inference

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Queue length approximates service cost for dense decode, but MoE requests with equal queue size can activate very different expert working sets. Task-agnostic placement then turns coactivation and cross-GPU weight traffic into hidden latency.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** TACG builds a task-conditioned expert coactivation graph for capacity-feasible placement, while GESR replicates generic experts and selects replicas using locality and load; this turns MoE expert placement and cross-GPU communication into runtime workload-aware scheduling state rather than a change to router or expert semantics. TACG owns a calibrated task-conditioned expert coactivation graph; GESR owns replica candidates; the serving scheduler owns capacity-feasible placement using topology, locality and live load. The model router continues to own token-to-expert semantics, so placement may not rewrite routing decisions to improve its metric. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01007v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3 Method``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01007v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01007v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01007v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Limitations and Future Work``。The simulated placement traces support communication reduction for the disclosed models, task mixtures and topology/calibration assumptions. They do not demonstrate end-to-end production latency, migration cost or stability under unseen tasks and changing expert popularity. 原文边界保持为：The evaluation uses simulated placement traces for the disclosed MoE models, task families, topology assumptions, and calibration budget; it does not demonstrate end-to-end production latency, dynamic migration cost, or robustness to unseen task mixes and changing expert popularity.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Workload-aware grouping can reduce traffic but requires profiling, replica memory and graph refresh. Drifted coactivation causes poor placement; over-replication reduces capacity; migration can erase simulated gains. Static placement remains the reliable branch for stable mixes or expensive movement.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** MoE scheduling evolves from queue cardinality to a task-conditioned expert working-set contract; the next pressure is closing the loop with measured latency and bounded migration. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-TASK-AWARE-MOE-GROUPING:start -->TACG builds a task-conditioned expert coactivation graph for capacity-feasible placement, while GESR replicates generic experts and selects replicas using locality and load; this turns MoE expert placement and cross-GPU communication into runtime workload-aware scheduling state rather than a change to router or expert semantics. 证据边界：The evaluation uses simulated placement traces for the disclosed MoE models, task families, topology assumptions, and calibration budget; it does not demonstrate end-to-end production latency, dynamic migration cost, or robustness to unseen task mixes and changing expert popularity.<!-- claim:SF-TASK-AWARE-MOE-GROUPING:end -->

<!-- review:SF-DEEP-RESEARCH-RUBRIC-RL:start -->
### Deep Research as Rubric for Reinforcement Learning

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** A free-form judge can reward plausible research prose without fixing which external claims must be supported. Reinforcement learning then optimizes a moving, opaque target rather than a reproducible research contract.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** DR-Rubric retrieves external evidence, synthesizes atomic verifiable constraints, scores policy outputs against that rubric, and feeds the result into group-relative reinforcement learning; rubric provenance, granularity, bootstrap version, and polarization therefore become reward-state and stopping-contract inputs rather than free-form judge text. The rubric pipeline owns an evidence snapshot, atomic constraints, provenance, granularity and bootstrap version; the scorer owns criterion results; GRPO owns grouped rollouts and advantage; the stopping controller owns polarization/collapse limits. The policy output cannot write its own rubric or promote retrieved text to truth. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01091v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3 The DR-Rubric Framework``；artifact 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01091v1 exact-v1 HTML § `B.2 Implementation Details`; no immutable commit inferred beyond the manuscript`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01091v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01091v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Conclusion``。The reported research-agent tasks show gains from evidence-derived atomic rubrics and also expose the later-bootstrap polarization limit. They do not show that retrieval is complete or fresh, that generated constraints are correct, or that self-bootstrapping monotonically improves reward quality. 原文边界保持为：Results are bounded to the reported research-agent tasks, models, search environment, and bootstrap depth; external evidence may be incomplete or stale, generated constraints can be wrong, and later bootstrap steps polarize and collapse rather than guaranteeing self-improvement.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Atomic rubrics improve attribution but add retrieval cost, rubric-generation error and reward-state versioning. Stale evidence or a wrong constraint can systematically train the policy away from the target; repeated bootstrap can collapse diversity. Human-authored or executable rewards remain preferable where authoritative criteria exist.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** The reward interface evolves from judge text to versioned evidence → constraint → score → stopping artifacts; the next pressure is independent rubric validation and contamination-safe refresh. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `Integrate`。

<!-- claim:SF-DEEP-RESEARCH-RUBRIC-RL:start -->DR-Rubric retrieves external evidence, synthesizes atomic verifiable constraints, scores policy outputs against that rubric, and feeds the result into group-relative reinforcement learning; rubric provenance, granularity, bootstrap version, and polarization therefore become reward-state and stopping-contract inputs rather than free-form judge text. 证据边界：Results are bounded to the reported research-agent tasks, models, search environment, and bootstrap depth; external evidence may be incomplete or stale, generated constraints can be wrong, and later bootstrap steps polarize and collapse rather than guaranteeing self-improvement.<!-- claim:SF-DEEP-RESEARCH-RUBRIC-RL:end -->

<!-- review:SF-LOCAL-MIXVR:start -->
### Local MixVR: Breaking the Communication-Sample Dependence in Distributed Learning

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Fully synchronous variance-reduced training repeatedly communicates across all samples, while naive local steps reduce communication at the price of client drift. The paper asks whether local momentum and mixed minibatch budgets can weaken the round dependence without discarding the optimization invariant.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Local MixVR combines local double momentum, minibatch budget mixing and drift correction to reduce communication-round dependence on total samples. Training workers own local iterates, double-momentum state and sampled minibatches; the optimizer protocol owns drift-correction terms, synchronization points and the mixed budget; the coordinator may aggregate only states from the declared round/seed identity. Neither a systems benchmark nor a scheduler may rewrite optimizer state from observed throughput. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01128v1 exact-v1 HTML § `Methods.``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01128v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01128v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01128v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Conclusions and Future Work``。The convergence analysis supports the stated communication-round dependence under its smoothness and noise assumptions, and the experiments test the algorithmic trend. It does not price accelerator collectives, stragglers, checkpoint traffic or memory at LLM scale. 原文边界保持为：Theory assumes stated smoothness/noise conditions and experiments do not price real accelerator collectives, stragglers or optimizer state at LLM scale.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Fewer rounds require extra momentum/correction state and tolerate more local staleness. Incorrect budget mixing or delayed participants can bias the correction; theory-valid work can still lose wall-clock time to skew and collectives. Ordinary synchronized variance reduction remains preferable when communication is cheap or reproducibility dominates.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** The change is from “every variance-reduction step is globally synchronized” to “local progress is admitted under an explicit drift budget”; the next pressure is mapping that budget to elastic topology and checkpoint recovery. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-LOCAL-MIXVR:start -->Local MixVR combines local double momentum, minibatch budget mixing and drift correction to reduce communication-round dependence on total samples. 证据边界：Theory assumes stated smoothness/noise conditions and experiments do not price real accelerator collectives, stragglers or optimizer state at LLM scale.<!-- claim:SF-LOCAL-MIXVR:end -->

<!-- review:SF-SKILLREVISE:start -->
### SkillRevise: Improving LLM-Authored Agent Skills via Trace-Conditioned Skill Revision

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Rewriting a skill from a final success/failure label is cheap but cannot identify which guidance caused the trajectory. Repeated global edits can damage tasks already solved without the skill.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** SkillRevise diagnoses execution traces, writes reusable principles into explicit memory, proposes bounded skill revisions, and promotes a revision only through utility-gated selection, turning skill text, trace evidence, revision rounds, and rollback candidates into versioned platform state. Trace diagnosis writes source-linked failure evidence and reusable principles; the reviser owns bounded candidate skill text; a utility gate owns promotion across affected tasks; the registry owns version, rollback and provenance. Tool permissions and environment effects remain with their executors, not with skill memory. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01139v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3 Method: SkillRevise``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01139v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01139v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01139v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Analysis and Discussion``。SkillsBench and ALFWorld experiments support bounded trace-driven revision for the tested model pairs and also show that guidance can harm already-solved tasks. They do not establish universal transfer, automated leakage exclusion or recovery from missing authority/observability. 原文边界保持为：The evaluated SkillsBench and ALFWorld tasks and tested model pairs do not establish universal transfer; skill guidance can harm tasks already solved without it, leakage was only manually audited, and bounded revision cannot repair missing tool authority or environment observability.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Diagnosis and utility evaluation reduce blind edits but cost extra executions and can overfit to a trace. A wrong locator stores the wrong principle; a narrow gate promotes regressions outside its set. Stable hand-authored skills remain appropriate when failure modes are known and test coverage is small.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Skill maintenance evolves from overwrite-in-place to trace evidence → candidate revision → utility-gated promotion → rollback; the next pressure is cross-model validity and capability-aware tests. 关系限定为 `Layering / Dependency`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- review:SF-RL-SHARED-PREFIX-REUSE:start -->
### Schedule-Level Shared-Prefix Reuse for LLM RL Training

<!-- review:SF-SPARSE-REPEATED-TRAINING:start -->
### When Data Is Scarce: Scaling Sparse Language Models with Repeated Training

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Dense Chinchilla-style allocation and one-pass token accounting are useful when unique data grows with compute. Under scarce data, repeated presentations saturate differently, while sparsity changes active and effective capacity; optimizing either axis alone can choose the wrong model.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** The sparse data-constrained scaling law jointly models unique-token volume, repetition, effective parameters, and sparsity, so repeated-epoch pretraining must choose model size and sparsity against a data-saturation boundary instead of applying dense Chinchilla allocation or sparsity gains independently. The training planner owns unique-token count, presentation count/epochs, active and dense-equivalent parameters, sparsity process and theoretical compute budget as one allocation record. The trainer owns observed loss and checkpoint lineage; the runtime owns wall-clock/hardware efficiency, which the fitted FLOP law may not overwrite. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01155v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3.1 Experimental setup``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01155v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01155v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3.1 Experimental setup``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01155v1 exact-v1 HTML § `6 Conclusion``。The fitted law and held-out scale/repetition experiments support a joint data-repetition-sparsity boundary over the disclosed 15M–960M active-parameter regime and token budgets. They do not establish frontier-mixture optima, robustness to token-quality/optimizer changes or real hardware savings. 原文边界保持为：The fitted law is empirical over the disclosed scale, corpus diversity, sparsity process, and compute regime; it does not prove the same optimum for frontier-scale mixtures, changing token quality, optimizer changes, or real hardware efficiency, which the paper explicitly separates from theoretical FLOPs.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Sparsity can extend effective capacity under repeated data but adds routing/implementation overhead and model-law estimation error. Extrapolation can over-size a model or repeat stale data past its useful boundary. Dense allocation and new-data acquisition remain valid when the fitted regime or hardware efficiency does not transfer.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Pretraining allocation evolves from parameters × unique tokens to parameters × sparsity × unique tokens × presentations; the next pressure is joining statistical scaling with measured system efficiency. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `Integrate`。

<!-- claim:SF-SPARSE-REPEATED-TRAINING:start -->The sparse data-constrained scaling law jointly models unique-token volume, repetition, effective parameters, and sparsity, so repeated-epoch pretraining must choose model size and sparsity against a data-saturation boundary instead of applying dense Chinchilla allocation or sparsity gains independently. 证据边界：The fitted law is empirical over the disclosed scale, corpus diversity, sparsity process, and compute regime; it does not prove the same optimum for frontier-scale mixtures, changing token quality, optimizer changes, or real hardware efficiency, which the paper explicitly separates from theoretical FLOPs.<!-- claim:SF-SPARSE-REPEATED-TRAINING:end -->
<!-- review:SF-SPARSE-REPEATED-TRAINING:end -->

<!-- review:SF-LAKEHOUSE-SKILL-OPTIMIZATION:start -->
### “Skill issues”: data-centric optimization of lakehouse agents

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Editing a skill prompt by inspection is adequate for a stable tool, but a coding agent that changes a lakehouse repository can appear fluent while leaving the wrong branch, files or commit state. Optimization must bind guidance to executable repository outcomes.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** The lakehouse study treats skills and environment files as versioned agent artifacts, generates task scenarios, verifies branch and commit end conditions, and optimizes from traceable failures, binding skill revisions to executable repository state rather than prose-only prompt changes. Skill text, environment files, generated scenarios, trace failures and candidate revisions are versioned artifacts. The optimizer may propose a revision; VCS and task validators own branch/commit and executable end-state facts; the skill registry promotes only a candidate linked to those receipts. The agent model never owns repository truth. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01185v1 exact-v1 HTML § `3.1. Architecture``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01185v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01185v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4. Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01185v1 exact-v1 HTML § `6. Conclusion and future work``。The 25-task lakehouse evaluation shows that scenario generation and trace-linked revision can improve the tested Claude Code workflow while retaining validation records. It does not establish transfer beyond that platform or show that branch/commit success proves semantic data correctness. 原文边界保持为：Results depend on one data platform, its generated scenarios, and the tested Claude-agent setting; the method does not establish transfer to other tools or prove that branch/commit verification captures semantic data correctness.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Executable checks improve auditability but add scenario-maintenance cost and can overfit skills to generated tasks. Weak validators produce false promotion; stale environment files produce false rejection. Manual skills and direct repository tests remain the baseline for small, stable integrations.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** The mechanism moves skills from prose configuration to release artifacts tied to repository state; the next pressure is cross-platform conformance and semantic data validation beyond file-level success. 关系限定为 `Layering / Dependency`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- review:SF-LOW-RESOURCE-SAFETY-ACTION:start -->
### Low-Resource Safety Failures Are Action Failures, Not Representation Failures

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** A refusal-rate gap can be diagnosed either as missing harmfulness features or as failure to turn available features into a refusal action. Conflating the two sends mitigation to representation learning even when the downstream action path is the failing component.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Cross-lingual analysis finds harmfulness representations can remain separable while refusal actions fail, moving the safety bottleneck from feature availability to downstream action realization. Activation directions and probe scores are sensor state owned by the diagnostic pipeline. The decoder/policy owns the refusal action, the language-conditioned routing gate owns intervention, and the safety evaluator owns matched benign/attacked outcomes. A separable activation never receives execution or release authority. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01196v1 exact-v1 HTML § `Our method is most selective.``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01196v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01196v1 exact-v1 HTML § `Experimental setup.``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01196v1 exact-v1 HTML § `6 Conclusion and Discussion``。Cross-lingual probes, calibrated routing and behavioral comparisons support a representation/action gap in the disclosed model-language slices. They do not prove a universal latent harmfulness direction, complete language coverage or that steering alone supplies a production-safe controller. 原文边界保持为：Activation separability is not sufficient for safe generation; the model/language set bounds the diagnosis and steering intervention.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Matched multilingual probes and closed-loop outcomes cost more than response-only refusal counts, and steering can over-refuse benign inputs or fail under drift. A probe false negative can hide risk; a false positive can deny service. Deterministic policy, human escalation and ordinary behavioral evaluation remain necessary coexistence paths.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** The evidence chain evolves from response text to internal sensor and finally effect-time action; the next pressure is continuous calibration across language, checkpoint and policy revision. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-LOW-RESOURCE-SAFETY-ACTION:start -->Cross-lingual analysis finds harmfulness representations can remain separable while refusal actions fail, moving the safety bottleneck from feature availability to downstream action realization. 证据边界：Activation separability is not sufficient for safe generation; the model/language set bounds the diagnosis and steering intervention.<!-- claim:SF-LOW-RESOURCE-SAFETY-ACTION:end -->
<!-- review:SF-LOW-RESOURCE-SAFETY-ACTION:end -->

<!-- review:SF-DISCOURSEFLIP:start -->
### DiscourseFlip: An Oblique Discourse-Level Opinion Manipulation Attack against Black-box Retrieval-Augmented Generation

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Lexical anomaly checks and isolated poison-snippet tests are reasonable for obvious corpus attacks, but a coherent document can manipulate the discourse trajectory while looking locally benign. Black-box RAG feedback makes that intent adaptive.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** DiscourseFlip organizes opinion-manipulation goals as a hierarchical attack-surface graph and uses an agentic action loop to revise seemingly coherent documents against black-box RAG retrieval and generation feedback, showing that corpus admission must govern discourse-level provenance and intent rather than only lexical anomaly or isolated poison snippets. The attack loop owns a hierarchical manipulation goal, candidate document revisions and observed retrieval/generation feedback. The corpus admission service owns document provenance, intent policy and acceptance; the retriever owns observed ranking, and the generator owns response state. Attack success cannot grant the attacker authority over corpus truth. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01212v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Methodology``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01212v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01212v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Experiment Setting``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01212v1 exact-v1 HTML § `7 Conclusion``。The constructed opinion corpus and black-box experiments show that discourse-level iterative poisoning can evade local intuition in the tested systems. They do not establish stealth against every provenance policy, generalize the author metrics to all RAG systems or supply a complete deterministic defense. 原文边界保持为：The attack success evidence is bounded to the constructed opinion corpus, black-box systems, surrogate feedback, prompts, and author metrics; it does not establish stealth against every provenance policy or that the proposed mitigation notes are a complete deterministic defense.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Discourse-aware admission catches broader intent but is harder to specify and risks censoring legitimate viewpoint diversity. A weak policy misses oblique campaigns; a broad semantic judge can create false positives and shared-model bias. Immutable trusted corpora and deterministic source allow-lists remain sound in closed domains.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** RAG security evolves from token/snippet scanning to provenance- and intent-aware corpus admission over document sequences; the next pressure is accountable policy that separates manipulation from legitimate disagreement. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-DISCOURSEFLIP:start -->DiscourseFlip organizes opinion-manipulation goals as a hierarchical attack-surface graph and uses an agentic action loop to revise seemingly coherent documents against black-box RAG retrieval and generation feedback, showing that corpus admission must govern discourse-level provenance and intent rather than only lexical anomaly or isolated poison snippets. 证据边界：The attack success evidence is bounded to the constructed opinion corpus, black-box systems, surrogate feedback, prompts, and author metrics; it does not establish stealth against every provenance policy or that the proposed mitigation notes are a complete deterministic defense.<!-- claim:SF-DISCOURSEFLIP:end -->
<!-- review:SF-DISCOURSEFLIP:end -->

<!-- review:SF-SKILLSMITH:start -->
### SkillSmith: Co-Evolving Skills and Tools for Self-Improving Agent Systems

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Maintaining skill instructions and executable tools in separate loops keeps boundaries simple, but a skill may demand a capability the tool no longer exposes, while a tool edit may invalidate procedures. Independent optimization can therefore create an unreleasable pair.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** SkillSmith co-evolves versioned skill instructions and executable tools through bounded atomic wrap/edit/compose/split/retire operations, validation-gated state updates, Pareto management, and anti-pattern memory, making tool capability and skill procedure one jointly governed release graph. Atomic wrap/edit/compose/split/retire operations create candidate skill/tool graph versions; validation and Pareto management own promotion evidence; anti-pattern memory records rejected lineage. The capability registry owns executable code and permissions, the skill registry owns procedure, and a joint release gate commits only compatible versions. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01314v1 exact-v1 HTML § `2 Preliminaries and Problem Formulation``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01314v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01314v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01314v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Conclusion and Limitations``。OfficeQA, SealQA and WildClawBench results support co-evolution under the stated libraries and atomic-operation assumptions. They do not prove arbitrary code correctness, safe permissions, monotonic improvement or theorem validity outside those assumptions and model-judge measurements. 原文边界保持为：Evidence is bounded to OfficeQA, SealQA, WildClawBench, the initial libraries, model judges, and stated atomic-operation assumptions; theorem claims rely on those assumptions, and validation scores do not prove safe permissions, arbitrary code correctness, or monotonic improvement in open environments.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Joint search can repair interface drift but greatly expands the mutation and validation surface. Judge error can promote unsafe code; coupled versions increase rollback and supply-chain cost. Separately curated tools and skills remain preferable where capability interfaces are stable or effects are high risk.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** The platform evolves from two configuration lists to one versioned capability/procedure release graph; the next pressure is sandboxed execution, permission review and deterministic conformance before promotion. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- review:SF-SABER-CODING-AGENT-SAFETY:start -->
### SABER: Benchmarking Operational Safety of LLM Coding Agents in Stateful Project Workspaces

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Response-level refusal is an adequate signal for advisory chat, but a coding agent can acknowledge danger and still alter a stateful workspace through a shortcut or multi-step effect. Operational safety must therefore evaluate environment state, not tone.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** Saber evaluates coding agents inside stateful workspaces with executable harm, local safe alternatives, rule-based violation checks, semantic auxiliary judging, and an outcome taxonomy that distinguishes refusal, recognition, unsafe shortcut, and multi-step environmental effects; the final workspace state, not response text, owns the operational-safety verdict. The sandbox owns initial and final workspace snapshots plus executable effects; deterministic rules own declared violations; the outcome taxonomy owns refusal/recognition/unsafe-shortcut categories; semantic judging is auxiliary. The agent may propose or execute only within the harness, and its final message cannot write the safety verdict. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01317v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Benchmark Design``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01317v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01317v1 exact-v1 HTML § `5 Experiments``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01317v1 exact-v1 HTML § `6 Conclusion``。The benchmark experiments establish distinct failure outcomes for the selected advisory-inspired templates, agents and tools. They do not make semantic judges infallible, show refusal is sufficient, cover every attack class or prove production containment outside the sandbox. 原文边界保持为：The benchmark covers its selected advisory-inspired templates, agents, tools, and sandbox; semantic judging remains auxiliary and fallible, refusal is not sufficient safety, and benchmark coverage does not establish production containment or exhaustive attack classes.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Stateful execution improves evidence but raises sandbox fidelity, cleanup and checker-maintenance cost. An incomplete rule misses harm; an unrealistic workspace overstates safety; a semantic judge can add correlated error. Static analysis and response-level tests remain useful prefilters, but not final effect evidence.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Coding-agent safety evolves from text refusal to executable scenario → workspace diff → rule/outcome verdict; the next pressure is production-equivalent containment and side-effect reconciliation. 关系限定为 `Direct Evolution`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-SABER-CODING-AGENT-SAFETY:start -->Saber evaluates coding agents inside stateful workspaces with executable harm, local safe alternatives, rule-based violation checks, semantic auxiliary judging, and an outcome taxonomy that distinguishes refusal, recognition, unsafe shortcut, and multi-step environmental effects; the final workspace state, not response text, owns the operational-safety verdict. 证据边界：The benchmark covers its selected advisory-inspired templates, agents, tools, and sandbox; semantic judging remains auxiliary and fallible, refusal is not sufficient safety, and benchmark coverage does not establish production containment or exhaustive attack classes.<!-- claim:SF-SABER-CODING-AGENT-SAFETY:end -->

<!-- review:SF-MEMORY-FRESHNESS-ASSEMBLY:start -->
### Don’t Ask the LLM to Track Freshness: A Deterministic Recipe for Memory Conflict Resolution

<!-- claim:SF-MEMORY-FRESHNESS-ASSEMBLY:start -->把 retrieval、语义过滤、冲突/新旧 policy 和生成合在一次 LLM call 中简单，却会让正确证据在组装阶段丢失。该接口先抽取 matching evidence 成候选结构，再由独立 policy executor 选择 current value。 MAB FactConsolidation、262K context、同 backbone/top-10 controlled comparison 与 LongMemEval negative check 支持收益主要来自阶段分离；不证明所有 memory task 有效。额外阶段增加 latency，且只有显式 version metadata 时 freshness policy 可审计。<!-- claim:SF-MEMORY-FRESHNESS-ASSEMBLY:end -->
Deep evidence contract：Method/identity=`arXiv:2606.01435v1 exact-v1 HTML § `§3 Method``；Evaluation=`arXiv:2606.01435v1 exact-v1 HTML § `§4 Experimental Setup``；Limitations/counterevidence=`arXiv:2606.01435v1 exact-v1 HTML § `§6.4 Limitations``；Artifact=`arXiv:2606.01435v1 exact-v1 HTML § `§4.6 Compute and reproducibility`; no immutable commit inferred beyond the manuscript`。Owner/authority：`Don’t Ask the LLM to Track Freshness: A Deterministic Recipe for Memory Conflict Resolution` 的长期机制归入 `AGENT-MEMORY`；experience/memory 的 source、derived state、freshness、conflict、delete 与 backend capability 必须分开；原始 trajectory/evidence 保留可追溯权。
<!-- review:SF-MEMORY-FRESHNESS-ASSEMBLY:end -->

<!-- review:SF-REASONING-PRODUCTION-EVAL-GAP:start -->
### An Enigma of Artificial Reason: Investigating the Production-Evaluation Gap in Large Reasoning Models

**问题、旧路径与约束变化。** Final-answer correctness is a useful outcome metric, but it can cause a judge to accept invalid intermediate reasoning after seeing the right answer. Production quality and evaluation quality must therefore be separated rather than inferred from one another.

**Mechanism、state/data/control owner 与实现。** The paper separates reasoning production from reasoning evaluation and identifies answer-confirmation bias: a valid final answer can cause evaluator models and process reward models to accept invalid intermediate reasoning, requiring an evaluation contract that independently binds step validity, answer validity, evaluator state, and causal diagnostics. The trace verifier owns step-validity judgments, the answer verifier owns final-answer validity, and the evaluation run owns judge/prompt/checkpoint state plus human/probe/causal-diagnostic receipts. Release logic may combine these states but may not let a valid answer overwrite an invalid step label. Method/identity 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01462v1 exact-v1 HTML § `Abstract``；artifact 定位为 `Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01462v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。未披露的实现 revision、runtime 或硬件路径不作补写。

**Evaluation、证明与未证明。** Evaluation 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01462v1 exact-v1 HTML § `2 Evaluating the Evaluation of Reasoning``；limitations/counterevidence 定位为 `arXiv:2606.01462v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 Discussion``。Constructed reasoning tasks, human comparisons, probes and causal patching support answer-confirmation bias in the tested large reasoning models and process evaluators. They do not show one universal causal failure or provide an unbiased production-ready verifier. 原文边界保持为：The evidence is bounded to the constructed reasoning tasks, tested large reasoning models, human comparison, probes, and causal-patching setup; it does not show that all process evaluators share one causal failure or provide a production-ready unbiased verifier.

**Trade-off、failure mode 与共存。** Independent step evaluation improves diagnosis but is expensive and can penalize valid alternative reasoning. Answer-hidden judging can lose necessary context; answer-visible judging can leak confirmation. Deterministic step checks remain preferable in formal domains, while answer-only evaluation remains a bounded metric when process validity is not claimed.

**Evolution 与系统位置。** Evaluation evolves from answer score to separately versioned answer, step and evaluator-state evidence; the next pressure is a verifier whose own causal failure modes are continuously audited. 关系限定为 `Layering / Dependency`；Books disposition 为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`。

<!-- claim:SF-REASONING-PRODUCTION-EVAL-GAP:start -->The paper separates reasoning production from reasoning evaluation and identifies answer-confirmation bias: a valid final answer can cause evaluator models and process reward models to accept invalid intermediate reasoning, requiring an evaluation contract that independently binds step validity, answer validity, evaluator state, and causal diagnostics. 证据边界：The evidence is bounded to the constructed reasoning tasks, tested large reasoning models, human comparison, probes, and causal-patching setup; it does not show that all process evaluators share one causal failure or provide a production-ready unbiased verifier.<!-- claim:SF-REASONING-PRODUCTION-EVAL-GAP:end -->
<!-- review:SF-REASONING-PRODUCTION-EVAL-GAP:end -->

### ClawHub Security Signals: When VirusTotal, Static Analysis, and SkillSpector Disagree

<!-- claim:SF-CLAWHUB-SECURITY-SIGNALS:start -->单一 malware scanner 的 allow/block 简单，但 Agent skill 同时包含脚本、指令与语义权限，VirusTotal、静态规则和 SkillSpector 覆盖面不同。该数据集把三类 signal 与 registry verdict 对齐，专门分析 disagreement。 67,453 个 sanitized latest skill、overlap 与 verdict slice 支持 layered triage；registry 是 automated silver label，不是 malicious prevalence 或 human truth。多 scanner 增加误报与审核成本，effect-time sandbox/least privilege 仍是最终控制。<!-- claim:SF-CLAWHUB-SECURITY-SIGNALS:end -->

Scanner Disagreement`; § `7. Verdict Structure and Risk Categories`; § `8. Illustrative Cases``；Limitations/counterevidence=`arXiv:2606.01494v1 exact-v1 HTML § `11. Threats to Validity`; § `13. Discussion``；Artifact=`arXiv:2606.01494v1 exact-v1 HTML § `12. <!-- review:SF-CROSS-INSTANCE-LATENT-REDISTRIBUTION:start -->
### Move the Query, Not the Cache: Characterizing Cross-Instance Latent and Sparse Attention Redistribution Across GPU Fabrics

<!-- claim:SF-CROSS-INSTANCE-LATENT-REDISTRIBUTION:start -->cross-instance sparse attention 通常搬 selected KV block；MLA 将每 token K/V 压成窄 latent 后，约 1KB query row 可能比 cache chunk 更小。该工作将 probe/transfer/compute/return/merge 分解成 cost model，按 fabric 与 request shape 决定 route-query、fetch-cache 或 local。 真实多节点 H100/IBGDA、批量 round-trip 与约 7% model error 支持这些测量系数；predicate 不是所有网络和架构的常数，re-adaptation splice 也有失败面。新架构需重测 payload/fetch 系数，单机或大 query 仍可搬 KV。<!-- claim:SF-CROSS-INSTANCE-LATENT-REDISTRIBUTION:end -->
Deep evidence contract：Method/identity=`arXiv:2606.01502v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4 A Topology-Aware Redistribution Cost Model`; § `5 Primitive Selection: route vs fetch vs local``；Evaluation=`arXiv:2606.01502v1 exact-v1 HTML § `4.3 Fit and validation on real IBGDA`; § `6 Characterizing the Device-Initiated RDMA Regime`; § `8 Sensitivity: Scaling and Topology``；Limitations/counterevidence=`arXiv:2606.01502v1 exact-v1 HTML § `10. Conclusion` — no dedicated limitations heading; the cited closing section was read and the Daily claim boundary records the source-specific non-result`；Artifact=`Not Disclosed — arXiv:2606.01502v1 exact-v1 HTML does not pin an immutable implementation revision`。Owner/authority：`Move the Query, Not the Cache: Characterizing Cross-Instance Latent and Sparse Attention Redistribution Across GPU Fabrics` 的长期机制归入 `INFER-PD-DISAGGREGATION`；route-query/fetch-KV 选择必须绑定 payload、fabric、topology epoch 与 transfer completion；远端 runtime 和 merge check 共同确认结果。

<!-- review:SF-AGENT-OPERATING-SYSTEM:start -->
### Agent Operating Systems (AOS): Integrating Agentic Control Planes into, and Beyond, Traditional Operating Systems

<!-- claim:SF-AGENT-OPERATING-SYSTEM:start -->传统 OS 的 process/thread/syscall 假设确定控制流和有界生命周期；长寿命 Agent 动态选工具、积累状态并跨 session 改变目标。AOS 架构在现有 OS 上增加 agent scheduler、context/memory、capability registry、policy/trust enforcement 与 audit control plane，并列出从 user space 到 distributed plane 的集成层级。 这是架构/非目标/评价标准分析，不是实现 benchmark；它支持责任分层，却不证明应由新 OS 取代 Linux/Windows。agent control plane 会扩大 TCB 和调度复杂度，普通应用仍应运行在现有 OS 隔离内。<!-- claim:SF-AGENT-OPERATING-SYSTEM:end -->

<!-- review:SF-SKILL-INJECTION-GUARDIAN:start -->
### Skill Injection Guardian

Skill 文档既是操作知识又是未信任输入。static guardian 在发布前重写，dynamic guardian 在读取时中介；两者降低所测三类 agent 的攻击成功率，却新增 guardian 自身的模型错误、重写语义漂移与运行成本。reframing 仍能改变攻击效果，说明“过滤一次”不是信任证明。<!-- claim:SF-SKILL-INJECTION-GUARDIAN:start -->可证明的是 Skill 必须经过 provenance、capability 与 runtime mediation；不能证明 guardian 单点足以替代 sandbox、least privilege 和 effect-level authorization。
<!-- claim:SF-SKILL-INJECTION-GUARDIAN:end -->
<!-- review:SF-SKILL-INJECTION-GUARDIAN:end -->

<!-- review:SF-ROBOTRUSTBENCH-WORLD-MODEL:start -->
### RoboTrustBench

只在可行、安全指令上评价生成视频，容易把视觉连贯误当作 world-model trust。该 benchmark 从真实 DROID 初态构造 normal、constraint-sensitive、counterfactual、adversarial 四类 instruction-image pair，并把 entity、时空、interaction、instruction 与 safety 拆成 13 项 human/MLLM criteria。<!-- claim:SF-ROBOTRUSTBENCH-WORLD-MODEL:start -->它支持“world-model evaluation 必须加入不可行与危险条件并区分视觉质量和物理/安全判断”；所有评估仍是离线生成视频，没有在真实机器人执行，也不能把 judge agreement 升级为 action-conditioned causal correctness。
<!-- claim:SF-ROBOTRUSTBENCH-WORLD-MODEL:end -->
<!-- review:SF-ROBOTRUSTBENCH-WORLD-MODEL:end -->

<!-- review:SF-OPTCC-ASYMMETRIC-ALLREDUCE:start -->
### OptCC

标准 ring 在某一 server NIC 降速后让所有 rank 受最慢 hop 约束。论文先给非对称带宽下界，再以四阶段 schedule 重排 reduce、scatter、allgather 与局部转发，使健康路径不必全程等待退化路径。<!-- claim:SF-OPTCC-ASYMMETRIC-ALLREDUCE:start -->证据支持“collective algorithm 必须消费当前 topology/bandwidth state”这一机制；它不证明无额外 buffer、控制开销或多故障下仍保持同样收益。旧 ring 在对称网络、故障直接 fail-stop 或恢复时间短时仍更简单。
<!-- claim:SF-OPTCC-ASYMMETRIC-ALLREDUCE:end -->
<!-- review:SF-OPTCC-ASYMMETRIC-ALLREDUCE:end -->

<!-- review:SF-GAIATRACE-VIDUR-AGENT:start -->
### GAIATrace / Vidur-Agent

传统 serving trace 以单 query 为单位，无法表达 agent task 内的依赖、tool latency 与多模型阶段。论文将 task/query/tool dependency 外置为可重放 trace，并扩展 simulator 以消费 KV/prefix cache、PD、routing 与 scheduling 配置。它证明所测两套 agent 的 task-level latency 与 TTFT/TPOT 会错位，也证明负载变化会移动瓶颈；没有证明两套 GAIA trace 能代表所有 agent workload，开源 artifact 在 v1 仍是发布承诺。 <!-- claim:SF-GAIATRACE-VIDUR-AGENT:start -->证据边界绑定 exact-v1 与上述 workload；不得把作者结果外推成跨模型/跨部署普遍结论。<!-- claim:SF-GAIATRACE-VIDUR-AGENT:end -->
<!-- review:SF-GAIATRACE-VIDUR-AGENT:end -->

<!-- review:SF-SPARSEX-SEGMENT-KV:start -->
### SparseX

Prefix cache 只复用完全相同的前缀，而真实 RAG/Agent 内容常以非前缀 segment 重现。SparseX 先对 segment 做 RoPE 对齐，再用 Sparse-Q 找需修正 token，在一次 forward 中重算局部 KV。<!-- claim:SF-SPARSEX-SEGMENT-KV:start -->论文证明的是所测模型/长度下 segment reuse 的可行性，不是任意切片均等价；segment identity 仍必须绑定 tokenization、position、model/adapter 与 correction policy，边界依赖或 selector drift 时要回退 dense Prefill。
<!-- claim:SF-SPARSEX-SEGMENT-KV:end -->
<!-- review:SF-SPARSEX-SEGMENT-KV:end -->

<!-- review:SF-ADAPTIVE-AUTO-HARNESS:start -->
### Adaptive Auto-Harness

单一 harness 在固定 benchmark 上反复优化时合理，但 open-ended task stream 会累积 history、domain shift 与 specialization conflict。Adaptive Auto-Harness 把持续 evolution、solve-time harness-tree routing 和 human steering 分成三个控制面，并保存 stateful evolution history。三类流式 benchmark 与 ablation 支持该分工在给定系统中减轻峰值后退化；不能证明自动演化不会 reward-hack，也没有把 human intervention、branch retirement、artifact provenance 与生产 rollback 全部闭合。<!-- claim:SF-ADAPTIVE-AUTO-HARNESS:start -->本报告只保留上述 exact-v1 机制、实验边界与 failure mode，不把作者 benchmark 外推为通用生产结论。<!-- claim:SF-ADAPTIVE-AUTO-HARNESS:end -->
<!-- review:SF-ADAPTIVE-AUTO-HARNESS:end -->

<!-- review:SF-CONSERVE-CONVERSATION-PLACEMENT:start -->
### ConServe

逐 turn 决定 PD placement 必须预测尚不可见的 output length、tool delay 与 KV growth。ConServe 把调度单位提升为 conversation：首轮长 Prefill 交给 prefiller，KV 只迁移一次，随后会话固定到一个 decoder；调度器只读取 first-turn input length 与 decoder KV occupancy。<!-- claim:SF-CONSERVE-CONVERSATION-PLACEMENT:start -->作者在四张 A40、Qwen3-0.6B replay 与 SWE-agent traces 上证明这一 owner change 可减少预测依赖；不证明所有 agent conversation 都有稳定两阶段结构，模型、工具或负载变化时仍需迁移、再平衡与 admission fallback。
<!-- claim:SF-CONSERVE-CONVERSATION-PLACEMENT:end -->
<!-- review:SF-CONSERVE-CONVERSATION-PLACEMENT:end -->

<!-- review:SF-COMPRESSION-UNCERTAINTY:start -->
### Compression Uncertainty

压缩评估通常只比较 accuracy/perplexity，却可能遗漏置信集合扩大与 selective-risk 变化。论文以 conformal prediction 在 12 个 LLM、量化/稀疏配置与五项任务上分离 accuracy 和 uncertainty，观察到规模依赖与阈值式 inflation。它证明 deployment gate 不能由 accuracy 单指标代理；不证明 conformal coverage 在 distribution shift、生成式开放答案或线上 calibration drift 下自动保持，也未给统一 latency/SLO。<!-- claim:SF-COMPRESSION-UNCERTAINTY:start -->本报告只保留上述 exact-v1 机制、实验边界与 failure mode，不把作者 benchmark 外推为通用生产结论。<!-- claim:SF-COMPRESSION-UNCERTAINTY:end -->
<!-- review:SF-COMPRESSION-UNCERTAINTY:end -->

<!-- review:SF-ASYNC-INFERENCE-OVERHEADS:start -->
### Albireo / Non-scalable Inference Overheads

扩大 Tensor Parallel degree 可以释放每卡 KV 空间，却会放大 scheduler、I/O、sampling 等不能随 GPU 数缩短的 host/runtime 比例。Albireo 将 scheduling 与 I/O 和 GPU compute 重叠，并把 sampling 沿 sequence parallel 化，以移动 Amdahl 最优点而不改模型。<!-- claim:SF-ASYNC-INFERENCE-OVERHEADS:start -->证据支持 inference scaling 必须分别计量 model-parallel compute、communication 与 non-scalable control path；作者基准和 production deployment 不能证明任意 runtime/模型都得到相同收益，异步 overlap 还引入 buffer lifetime、ordering、backpressure 与 late-result failure mode。
<!-- claim:SF-ASYNC-INFERENCE-OVERHEADS:end -->
<!-- review:SF-ASYNC-INFERENCE-OVERHEADS:end -->

<!-- review:SF-DRIFT-TELBENCH:start -->
### DRIFT / TELBench

final-answer evaluator 只能看到失败结果，不能定位 trajectory 中第一次有害承诺。TELBench 将 2,790 条轨迹切成 semantic spans，并建立 1,000-instance localization benchmark；DRIFT 再按 claim 跟踪 evidence、support 与传播。<!-- claim:SF-DRIFT-TELBENCH:start -->证据支持 outcome → span → claim propagation 的诊断分层，但 LLM-assisted annotation、framework/backbone mix 与 benchmark construction 不能证明 locator 找到真实因果根因；它是 failure sensor，不是 truth owner。
<!-- claim:SF-DRIFT-TELBENCH:end -->
<!-- review:SF-DRIFT-TELBENCH:end -->

<!-- review:SF-DFLARE-DIFFUSION-SPECULATION:start -->
### DFlare

Block diffusion speculation 同时提议一块 token，旧 draft 让所有 draft layer 共享少数 target-layer 融合表示，形成容量瓶颈。DFlare 让每个 draft layer 学习自己的多 target-layer fusion，并扩大 draft depth 与训练数据，再由 target 并行验证。<!-- claim:SF-DFLARE-DIFFUSION-SPECULATION:start -->六类 benchmark 支持“draft capacity 与 conditioning interface 共同限制 acceptance/speed”这一实验性分支；结果绑定三种 target、训练数据和作者实现，不能外推为 diffusion 解码普遍优于 AR，也不能省略 target verification、rollback 与 distribution/quality contract。
<!-- claim:SF-DFLARE-DIFFUSION-SPECULATION:end -->
<!-- review:SF-DFLARE-DIFFUSION-SPECULATION:end -->

<!-- review:SF-STRAGGLER-AWARE-RL-GROUP:start -->
### Straggler-Aware Group Control

固定 rollout group 保留同步 on-policy 与组内 normalization，但 group 越大，极长 rollout 阻塞 reward/update 的概率越高。SAGC 把 group size 作为在线受约束控制变量，根据 observed rollout behavior 与长期 straggler-rate risk 调整，而不是改成无界 stale async。<!-- claim:SF-STRAGGLER-AWARE-RL-GROUP:start -->GRPO/DAPO 实验支持动态 group 可以在所测训练栈改善 wall-clock 且保持 reward/quality；不能证明 controller 在 workload shift、reward drift 或不同 rollout service 下仍校准。最小固定组在负载稳定、可预测性优先时仍更简单。
<!-- claim:SF-STRAGGLER-AWARE-RL-GROUP:end -->
<!-- review:SF-STRAGGLER-AWARE-RL-GROUP:end -->

<!-- review:SF-SECLAW-SPEC-DRIVEN-SECURITY:start -->
### SeClaw

静态攻击 prompt 与 final-answer judge 看不到 agent 在文件、权限、Skill/MCP 与命令执行中的中间副作用。SeClaw exact-v1 提出用结构化 risk/deployment/tool spec 生成任务，在 Docker 中重建执行环境并记录 trajectory；但稿件标题已明确是 preliminary/in-progress，正文只给出框架与后续评测计划，没有跨模型/跨 harness 的结果或独立 limitations 章节。因此它只能作为现有 security EvalSpec 思路的早期实现案例，不能证明该流程已形成可复现 benchmark，也不构成新的 Books 机制。 <!-- claim:SF-SECLAW-SPEC-DRIVEN-SECURITY:start -->证据边界绑定 exact-v1 与上述 workload；不得把作者结果外推成跨模型/跨部署普遍结论。<!-- claim:SF-SECLAW-SPEC-DRIVEN-SECURITY:end -->
<!-- review:SF-SECLAW-SPEC-DRIVEN-SECURITY:end -->

<!-- review:SF-HARNESS1-EXTERNALIZED-STATE:start -->
### Harness-1

纯 transcript policy 同时承担搜索决策与候选池、证据链接、verification record、压缩摘要等可恢复 bookkeeping。Harness-1 让环境拥有这些状态，policy 通过 edit actions 改写；这缩短 policy 需要隐式记忆的控制面。<!-- claim:SF-HARNESS1-EXTERNALIZED-STATE:start -->实验只证明特定 20B agent、search tasks 与训练配方下的收益；harness state 仍可能 stale、被错误压缩或与网页事实分叉，因此必须 version、provenance、rebuild，并保留 final verification。
<!-- claim:SF-HARNESS1-EXTERNALIZED-STATE:end -->
<!-- review:SF-HARNESS1-EXTERNALIZED-STATE:end -->

<!-- review:SF-LLMFI-ERROR-PROPAGATION:start -->
### LLMFI

只对 final token 或 aggregate accuracy 做 fault test，会掩盖 fault 在 layer、operation、token 与 task 间如何传播。LLMFI 以确定性 fault injection 在三种 open-weight LLM、13 类任务中定位 resilience pattern，并区分检测与软件 mitigation。<!-- claim:SF-LLMFI-ERROR-PROPAGATION:start -->该研究支持 evaluation subject 必须绑定 injection site、bit/error model、model/task 与 observable outcome；模拟 fault 不给出现实发生率，也不证明作者四种 mitigation 覆盖 GPU、network、kernel 与 checkpoint 的真实故障分布。
<!-- claim:SF-LLMFI-ERROR-PROPAGATION:end -->
<!-- review:SF-LLMFI-ERROR-PROPAGATION:end -->

<!-- review:SF-PEFT-SCALE:start -->
### PEFT Scale Up / Down / Out

论文把 adapter 从“便宜微调”重述为共享 base 上的持久局部状态，并区分更强 prior、adapter 最小可靠容量、百万实例治理三轴。<!-- claim:SF-PEFT-SCALE:start -->它支持 adapter identity/revision/provenance/evaluation/residency 必须分层，但不证明每个用户都应持有 adapter，也不能把 population 数量等同同时驻留数量。现有 Model Registry 章节已经拥有该长期合同，故不重复写入。
<!-- claim:SF-PEFT-SCALE:end -->
<!-- review:SF-PEFT-SCALE:end -->

<!-- review:SF-GHOST-TOOL-ISSUE-PRIVACY:start -->
### Ghost Tool Calls

Speculative tool dispatch 即使 read-only、最终放弃，也已把推断出的用户意图交给外部 observer；commit-time cleanup 无法撤回 disclosure。论文把“被观察”从 state mutation 中分离为 issue-time effect，并让 policy 在发送前抑制或投影 argument/destination。<!-- claim:SF-GHOST-TOOL-ISSUE-PRIVACY:start -->三套 corpus、12 种 policy 的原型支持 privacy boundary 必须在 dispatch 前执行；它不证明 prototype 覆盖真实 provider retention、timing side channel 或多工具 workflow。低风险本地 pure function 可继续 speculation，外部 observer 则需 issue-time contract。
<!-- claim:SF-GHOST-TOOL-ISSUE-PRIVACY:end -->
<!-- review:SF-GHOST-TOOL-ISSUE-PRIVACY:end -->

<!-- review:SF-SKILLHARM-LIFECYCLE:start -->
### SkillHarm

一次 session 的 poisoned skill 测试会漏掉 persistent package 被静默改写后在未来复用触发的 harm。SkillHarm 把 fixed-payload 与 self-mutating poisoning 放进 skill lifecycle，并按 data、environment、autonomy 构造 879 个攻击样本。作者结果显示当前 defenses 在该 harness 下仍脆弱；ASR 受 agent 是否读取 poisoned file 影响，未触发不等于抵抗，benchmark 也不能证明所有 skill ecosystem 的真实发生率。<!-- claim:SF-SKILLHARM-LIFECYCLE:start -->本报告只保留上述 exact-v1 机制、实验边界与 failure mode，不把作者 benchmark 外推为通用生产结论。<!-- claim:SF-SKILLHARM-LIFECYCLE:end -->
<!-- review:SF-SKILLHARM-LIFECYCLE:end -->

<!-- audit-target:evidence:end -->

<!-- analysis:DA-DIFFUSION-COMMIT:start -->
### DA-DIFFUSION-COMMIT: Decoding in Order-Agnostic Language Models: Chain-Rule Deviation and Uniform Spreading

**Why.** 自回归生成把已提交 prefix 当作一个有明确 chain-rule 语义的不可变历史；masked/order-agnostic 生成允许多个位置反复变化，因此 reveal order 不再只是调度细节。

**Principle.** 并行更新只有在 conditionals 能组成一致 joint、且 provisional 与 committed state 被区分时，才能把吞吐收益与输出语义同时解释。

**Mechanism.** exact-v1 分析测量不同 reveal order 下的 chain-rule deviation 与 probability spreading，把解码顺序提升为 generation contract；它影响 correction、cache validity、streaming 与最终 commit。

**Trade-off and evidence boundary.** 允许修改未提交位置能并行探索和纠错，却增加重算、振荡、停止规则与commit bookkeeping。作者结果只覆盖测试模型和 schedule，不证明全部 diffusion LM 不一致，也不证明 AR 普遍更优。

**Connection and evolution.** 这条路线从 append-only AR，演进到 mutable masked state，再要求显式 commit protocol；后续压力是让训练 objective、runtime cache 与外部副作用共享同一提交语义。

**Source-family boundary.** Order-agnostic masked language models expose conditionals that need not compose into one coherent joint distribution; reveal order therefore becomes part of the decoding contract rather than a harmless scheduler choice. 证据边界：Reported likelihood shifts and uniform-spreading effects are specific to evaluated models and schedules; they do not prove that every diffusion language model is incoherent or inferior to autoregression.
<!-- analysis:DA-DIFFUSION-COMMIT:end -->

<!-- analysis:DA-FAIL-CLOSED-KV:start -->
### DA-FAIL-CLOSED-KV: Fail-Closed Lowering of Resident KV Claims onto LLM Serving Runtimes

**Why.** Agentic request 会携带工具结果、adapter、position rule 与多轮前缀；只用 prefix hash 命中 KV，可能在数值可读但语义身份已变化时错误复用。

**Principle.** 缓存复用是 correctness claim，不是纯性能提示。不能证明 compatibility 时必须 miss，而不能先复用再依赖下游质量检测补救。

**Mechanism.** resident-KV claim 将 model/tokenizer/adapter/position/prefix 与 runtime revision 绑定为可验证 directive，由 serving runtime 在 admission 和 commit 前检查；Leyline 等协议只承担传输，最终 authority 仍在拥有物理 KV 的 runtime。

**Trade-off and evidence boundary.** 更强 identity 减少 silent corruption，却增加 metadata、hash、miss rate 和跨节点协调。论文没有证明所有实现的 tail-SLO 收益，协议字段也不能替代实际 memory ownership。

**Connection and evolution.** 路线从进程内前缀复用，演进到跨请求 directive，再到 fail-closed compatibility；下一压力是让迁移、PD 分离与多租户权限在同一 claim lifecycle 上原子更新。

**Source-family boundary.** priority、TTL、offload、event 与 KV-aware routing 是有用 substrate，但各自不承诺未来 reuse。ResidentClaim lowering 要求 accepted claim identity、materialization predicate、ordered lifecycle 与 claim-scoped outcome，并用 descriptor/checker 将 mapping 分成 native、adapter evidence、approximation、reject 或 unknown。 对 TensorRT-LLM、SGLang/HiCache、Dynamo 的 anchored descriptor 与 patched vLLM failure path 支持 semantics boundary；checker 不证明未审 runtime 完整，也没有 performance claim。claim metadata 与 fail-closed path 有成本，不需未来义务时弱 primitive 仍足够。
<!-- analysis:DA-FAIL-CLOSED-KV:end -->

<!-- analysis:DA-20260602-AGENT-SLO:start -->
### Agent workload 把 query SLO 推进为 task/effect evidence

Agent 系统不再能用单次 LLM query 的 TTFT、文本 refusal 或 final answer 代表整体正确性。trace-driven serving 把 task dependency 暴露给 scheduler；security evaluation 则把 tool/runtime/filesystem effect 暴露给 release Gate。共同变化是 evidence owner 从 transcript 移到可重放的 task graph 与 environment state，代价是 trace schema、sandbox fidelity 与 predicate coverage 成为新的 failure mode。
<!-- analysis:DA-20260602-AGENT-SLO:end -->

<!-- existing:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:start -->`PLATFORM-PRODUCTION` 当前与该机制最接近的命题是：本章只收束前 16 章已推导的机制，没有引入新的产品清单。自检答案回填把 demo 成功转换为 identity、quality、capacity、reliability、governance、economics 与 evolution 七类 proof obligations。它明确向 Part VII 交付第 66 章的 evaluation evidence/decision contract，以及 identity、policy、trace、budget、security 与 recovery contracts，避免 Agent 平台另起一套治理系统。 相邻章节对 handoff 的约束是：主体包括用户、service accounts、controllers、training code、model server、model output 和 external tools。模型生成的文本不是可信主体，也不应自动获得调用者全部权限。 / 随着规则增长，维护流程也会从 append-only 演进为 typed consolidation：先把 trigger、workflow edge、 tool argument、exception、stop condition 与 output field 抽成 contract，再区分重复、修订、新增和需要 局部重构的 patch。压缩目标不是“最短文本”，而是在保留稀有 guard 和不确定原文的前提下，消除重复 表达；结构检查只能证明抽取出的 contract 没有丢失，不能证明任意自然语言改写在所有模型、task 与 decoding 条件下行为等价，最终仍要经过本章前述 regression 与 canary。<!-- existing:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:end -->

<!-- delta:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:start -->Metastable faults arise from circular destabilizing interactions among individually stabilizing components; tentative scheduling of destabilizing actions and deferral until global stabilization changes the recovery contract from symptom mitigation to composition-aware liveness control. 证据边界：The formal result is relative to the paper's system model and adversaries, and the three case studies do not prove that the methodology detects every production feedback loop or that Nyx models preserve every implementation detail.<!-- delta:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:start -->`Characterizing Metastable Faults and Failures` 与 `PLATFORM-PRODUCTION` 的关系判定为 `Direct Evolution`。比较 `books/part-06-ai-infrastructure/73-production-best-practice.md#L201` 与 `books/part-06-ai-infrastructure/72-security.md#L27;books/part-07-agent/74-prompt.md#L141` 后，决定为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`：该 exact-v1 的机制 `Metastable faults arise from circular destabilizing interactions among individually stabilizing components; tentative scheduling of destabilizing actions and deferral until global stabilization changes the recovery contract from symptom mitigation to composition-aware liveness control.` 已被现有命题或相邻 handoff 覆盖；作者结果只增加受限实例，而 `The formal result is relative to the paper's system model and adversaries, and the three case studies do not prove that the methodology detects every production feedback loop or that Nyx models preserve every implementation detail.` 阻止把它提升为新的长期设计结论。 不把论文名称、作者 benchmark 或未披露实现写成长期机制；若为 Integrate，正文写回仍由 root 按日期串行处理。<!-- books-review:SF-METASTABLE-FAULT-SCHEDULING:end -->

<!-- existing:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:start -->`TRAIN-LORA` 当前与该机制最接近的命题是：本章使用 `W_0` 表示冻结的基座权重，`Delta W` 表示任务更新，`d_in`、`d_out` 表示线性层输入输出维度，`r` 表示 LoRA rank，`alpha` 表示缩放系数。 相邻章节对 handoff 的约束是：共享同一 perception state 采样多条 reasoning continuation，可用下游成功率估计该 perception 是否仍支持求解；再把 teacher-student aware-span disagreement 作为第二 witness，才允许在固定 distillation budget 内重分配 perception token 权重。两者都不是 calibrated posterior：teacher 可能共同出错，PSR 随 policy 漂移；reasoning objective 必须保持独立 owner。 / 这把 reward 从无状态 scalar service 变成与 policy coordinate 绑定的 measurement state，新增 hidden-space compatibility、representation privacy、version skew 和 policy/RM co-adaptation。冻结 RM 在 auditability、 低频 policy 更新或跨模型复用优先时仍合理；只有 drift 能被独立评估、head update 有 held-out gate 时才值得 动态追踪。作者 benchmark 不能证明 co-adaptation 已消除 reward hacking。<!-- existing:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:end -->

<!-- delta:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:start -->直接在 LoRA 的 A/B 因子上做 DP-SGD，在固定参数化下看似自然，但同一低秩更新存在多种 gauge 等价分解，噪声会被双线性放大。PRISM 改在低秩矩阵的内禀切空间采样 gauge-invariant 噪声，并让自适应更新不再放大该噪声。 形式化 DP 保证与数值实验支持“扰动应绑定可辨识更新而非任意因子”的边界；它不证明所有 rank、optimizer 和 privacy budget 下都优于普通 LoRA。内禀采样与几何计算增加实现复杂度，无隐私要求或 factor gauge 已固定时旧方案仍更简单。<!-- delta:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:end -->

<!-- books-review:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:start -->`PRISM: Gauge-Invariant Tangent-Space Differentially Private LoRA` 与 `TRAIN-LORA` 的关系判定为 `Layering / Dependency`。比较 `books/part-04-training-system/30-lora.md#L16` 与 `books/part-04-training-system/29-sft.md#L257;books/part-04-training-system/31-rlhf.md#L245` 后，决定为 `No Change — Existing Coverage`：该 exact-v1 的机制 `直接在 LoRA 的 A/B 因子上做 DP-SGD，在固定参数化下看似自然，但同一低秩更新存在多种 gauge 等价分解，噪声会被双线性放大。PRISM 改在低秩矩阵的内禀切空间采样 gauge-invariant 噪声，并让自适应更新不再放大该噪声。 形式化 DP 保证与数值实验支持“扰动应绑定可辨识更新而非任意因子”的边界；它不证明所有 rank、optimizer 和 privacy budget 下都优于普通 LoRA。内禀采样与几何计算增加实现复杂度，无隐私要求或 factor gauge 已固定时旧方案仍更简单。` 已被现有命题或相邻 handoff 覆盖；作者结果只增加受限实例，而 `直接在 LoRA 的 A/B 因子上做 DP-SGD，在固定参数化下看似自然，但同一低秩更新存在多种 gauge 等价分解，噪声会被双线性放大。PRISM 改在低秩矩阵的内禀切空间采样 gauge-invariant 噪声，并让自适应更新不再放大该噪声。 形式化 DP 保证与数值实验支持“扰动应绑定可辨识更新而非任意因子”的边界；它不证明所有 rank、optimizer 和 privacy budget 下都优于普通 LoRA。内禀采样与几何计算增加实现复杂度，无隐私要求或 factor gauge 已固定时旧方案仍更简单。` 阻止把它提升为新的长期设计结论。 不把论文名称、作者 benchmark 或未披露实现写成长期机制；若为 Integrate，正文写回仍由 root 按日期串行处理。<!-- books-review:SF-PRISM-DP-LORA:end -->

<!-- existing:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:start -->`INFER-SCHEDULING` 当前与该机制最接近的命题是：这是一种受 SLO 约束的 alternative branch，不是训练 scheduler 接管 inference。Inference runtime 仍拥有 request admission、batch 和 KV；training runtime 拥有 optimizer、gradient 和 adapter commit；co-serving controller 只拥有二者之间的瞬时资源租约。它增加 latency-model drift、CUDA graph/eager path 差异、activation residency、preemption granularity 和故障隔离风险。只有在实际 trace、目标 engine、adapter workload 和 tail-SLO 下重新校准后，才能把空闲算力视为可用容量。 相邻章节对 handoff 的约束是：最初的 PD 数据面通常把所有 KV movement 都交给同一条路径：Prefill miss 时从存储读取 prefix，Prefill 完成后再把新 KV 传给 Decode。这个设计在 cache hit 不高、请求 turn 少或网络带宽富余时最简单，identity、 retry 与回收也只有一套状态机。可是在长多轮 Agent workload 中，短增量 prompt 可能反复命中大 prefix；此时 Prefill compute 下降，storage-to-Prefill 的 read traffic 却未同比下降，原本为 P→D handoff 规划的 NIC/PCIe 路径会与 cache restore 争用。 / 因此 replica identity、coverage、failover 与 hot-shard policy 仍由 controller/workload owner 管理。小集群、每个 replica 必须拥有完整视图或版本不兼容时，full watch 仍最简单。这条演进说明平台控制面不仅要声明 desired state，还要明确谁验证状态、谁分配状态视图，以及恢复时怎样证明没有 gap、overlap 或静默漏事件。<!-- existing:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:end -->

<!-- delta:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:start -->round-robin、queue-aware 和 prefix-cache-aware routing 在请求分布与 GPU 同质时合理；agentic prompt、batch/KV 耦合和异构加速器使静态启发式失准。Lodestar 按请求采集 instance/request/performance snapshot，在线训练 reward predictor，再以预测 TTFT 选择实例。 公有云 GPU 实验与 prefix/load heuristic 的比较支持特定 workload 下的 TTFT 改善和约五分钟适应速度；它不证明 predictor 在突变、观测延迟或 reward drift 下仍安全。在线训练、探索和隔离带来 control-plane 成本，冷启动与失准时应回退确定性 router。<!-- delta:SF-LODESTAR-ONLINE-ROUTER:end -->
